Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: plank.

The lowering half of the dragon flag. You control a straight body down toward the bench, which builds serious core strength.

Complete ordered source instructions

  2. Raise your body into a straight vertical line on your upper back. 4982.json#/instructions/1

4984 — kneeling modified hindu push up

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4984 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: plank.

An easier knee-supported version of the hindu push up. The flowing dip and sweep trains the chest, shoulders and triceps and opens the spine.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a high-plank blocking pose on the documented support, with a long aligned torso; preserve any source-defined low-plank or lifted-leg modification.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Start on your knees and hands with hips high, like a pike. 4984.json#/instructions/0
  2. Bend your elbows and swoop your chest down and forward close to the floor. 4984.json#/instructions/1
  3. Push through your arms to glide your chest up and forward, arching slightly. 4984.json#/instructions/2
  4. Reverse the path, pushing your hips back up to the start. 4984.json#/instructions/3
  5. Move smoothly and keep your core engaged. 4984.json#/instructions/4

4985 — band assisted floor glute ham raise

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4985 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: kneeling.

Floor glute ham raise where a band helps you come back up. It builds the hamstrings and glutes with a manageable load.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Anchor a band at head height and loop it around your chest or hold it to your chest. Kneel with your feet held firmly under a stable object.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 4985.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Anchor a band at head height and loop it around your chest or hold it to your chest. 4985.json#/instructions/0
  2. Kneel with your feet held firmly under a stable object. 4985.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lower your torso forward slowly with a straight line from knees to head. 4985.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pull yourself back up with your hamstrings and glutes, using the band to help. 4985.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your hips extended throughout. 4985.json#/instructions/4

4986 — dumbbell step up v. 2

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 4986 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Step up onto a bench or box holding dumbbells. It trains your legs and glutes one side at a time.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Hold a dumbbell in each hand and stand facing a sturdy bench. Place one foot fully on the bench.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 4986.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Hold a dumbbell in each hand and stand facing a sturdy bench. 4986.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place one foot fully on the bench. 4986.json#/instructions/1
  3. Drive through that heel to stand up tall on the bench. 4986.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lower your trailing foot back down under control. 4986.json#/instructions/3
  5. Finish your reps, then switch legs. 4986.json#/instructions/4

Step back into a lunge while holding a kettlebell. Stepping backward is kind on the knees and loads the glutes and quads.

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 5108 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

Squat down and swing a dumbbell diagonally across your body. It trains legs, trunk rotation and shoulders in one movement.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with feet shoulder-width apart, holding a dumbbell with both hands by one hip. Sit into a squat. Stand up and swing the dumbbell diagonally across to overhead on the other side.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with feet shoulder-width apart, holding a dumbbell with both hands by one hip. 5108.json#/instructions/0
  2. Sit into a squat. 5108.json#/instructions/1
  3. Stand up and swing the dumbbell diagonally across to overhead on the other side. 5108.json#/instructions/2
  4. Rotate your torso and pivot your back foot as you go. 5108.json#/instructions/3
  5. Return and repeat, then switch sides. 5108.json#/instructions/4
